HRS 2022 Early Exit

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquestions@umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

XST149                        DEATH EXPENSES - MIN
         Section: T     Level: Respondent      Type: Numeric    Width: 6   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecT.EXITWILLANDDEATH.T149_

         (Thinking about the total expenses associated with the death for things like the
         funeral, legal fees and so on
         
         Do not include health care expenses:)
         
         Did it amount to less than $____ , more than $____ , or what?
         
         PROCEDURES: 2Up1Down, 1Up2Down
         BREAKPOINTS: $1,000, $5,000, $25,000, $100,000
         RANDOM ENTRY POINTS: $5,000, $25,000
         ENTRY POINT ASSIGNMENT 1 or {NOT 1} AT X505
         ORDER OF ENTRY POINT ASSIGNMENTS AND PROCEDURES CORRESPOND

         .................................................................................
           116           0.  Value of Breakpoint
             1        1000.  Value of Breakpoint
            40        1001.  Value of Breakpoint
            11        5000.  Value of Breakpoint

             3       25000.  Value of Breakpoint
            11       25001.  Value of Breakpoint
             1      100001.  Value of Breakpoint
           938       Blank.  INAP (Inapplicable); Partial Interview

         [{NOT 1 and NOT 2} ($500,000)] AT X511
